We evaluate two CLIP architectures spanning a 7x visual-encoder scale gap (XLM-R base + ViT-B/32, ~87M visual parameters; XLM-R large + ViT-H/14, ~632M) on 11 concepts and 210 images, and quantify cross-language agreement via cluster-mask IoU, top-percentile IoU, and Spearman rank correlation against an English reference (n=2,310 paired observations per language). Three findings emerge. First, low-resource languages (Arabic, Basque, Luxembourgish) incur a structural penalty at both backbone scales (Wilcoxon HR>LR p<10^-300; cluster-mask IoU gap +0.114 at base, +0.143 at large), isolating the deficit to the text branch. Second, scaling the encoder 7x widens the gap for structural failure cases (Basque Δ=-0.056, Luxembourgish Δ=-0.076) while improving Arabic (Δ=+0.033), separating corpus-coverage from tokeniser-fertility failures. Third, peak similarity is preserved across languages (mean ratio 0.94 at large scale) while cluster-mask IoU drops sharply, identifying spatial misalignment, not signal collapse, as the dominant failure mode. At 3.4-3.9 Wh per 1,000 queries, dense-CLIP grounding is competitive with high-throughput inference budgets, positioning it as a practical substrate for energy-aware multilingual deployment.

</details>


### [110] [Soohak: A Mathematician-Curated Benchmark for Evaluating Research-level Math Capabilities of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09063)
*Guijin Son,Seungone Kim,Catherine Arnett,Hyunwoo Ko,Hyein Lee,Hyeonah Kang,Jiang Longxi,Jin Yun,JungYup Lee,Kyungmin Lee,Sam Yoosuk Kim,Sang Park,Seunghyeok Hong,SeungJae Lee,Seungyeop Yi,Shinae Shin,SunHye Bok,Sunyoung Shin,Yonghoon Ji,Youngtaek Kim,Hanearl Jung,Akari Asai,Graham Neubig,Sean Welleck,Youngjae Yu,Akshelin R,Alexander B. Ivanov,Boboev Muhammadjon,Chaeyoung Han,Christian Stump,Dmitrii Karp,Dohyun Kwon,DoYong Kwon,Duk-Soon Oh,Giovanni Resta,Greta Panova,Huiyun Noh,Hyungryul Baik,Hyungsun Bae,Inomov Mashrafdzhon,Jeewon Kim,Ji Eun Lee,Jiaqi Liu,Jieui Kang,Jimin Kim,Jon-Lark Kim,Junseo Yoon,Junwoo Jo,Kibeom Kim,Kiwoon Kwon,Mario Kummer,Max Mercer,Minjun Kim,Nahyun Lee,Ng Ze-An,Rafał Marcin Łochowski,Raphaël Lachièze-Rey,Ruichen Zhang,Sejin Park,Seonguk Seo,Shin Jaehoon,Sunatullo,Taewoong Eom,Yeachan Park,Yongseok Jang,Youchan Oh,Zhaoyang Wang,Zoltán Kovács*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Soohak是一个由64位数学家从头编写的439个研究级数学问题基准，用于评估下一代前沿模型的推理能力。在Challenge子集上，顶级模型准确率仅为10.4%-30.4%，而开源模型低于15%。该基准还引入了refusal子集，测试模型识别不适定问题并拒绝回答的能力，但没有模型超过50%。数据集将于2026年底公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奥赛级问题仅衡量逐步推理能力，而研究级问题能衡量推动数学知识前沿的推理能力，但此类基准稀缺（如Riemann Bench和FrontierMath-Tier 4仅有25和50个问题）。为支持下一代前沿模型的可靠评估，需要更大规模的研究级数学基准。

Method: 由64位数学家从头编写439个研究级数学问题，分为Challenge子集（标准问题求解）和Refusal子集（测试模型识别不适定问题并拒绝回答的能力）。数据集将延迟至2026年底发布以防止污染。

Result: Challenge子集上，Gemini-3-Pro 30.4%、GPT-5 26.4%、Claude-Opus-4.5 10.4%，开源模型低于15%。Refusal子集上，所有模型均未超过50%。

Conclusion: Soohak为下一代模型提供了有显著提升空间的基准，同时首次将"拒绝回答"能力确立为当前模型未直接优化的新目标。

Abstract: Following the recent achievement of gold-medal performance on the IMO by frontier LLMs, the community is searching for the next meaningful and challenging target for measuring LLM reasoning. Whereas olympiad-style problems measure step-by-step reasoning alone, research-level problems use such reasoning to advance the frontier of mathematical knowledge itself, emerging as a compelling alternative. Yet research-level math benchmarks remain scarce because such problems are difficult to source (e.g., Riemann Bench and FrontierMath-Tier 4 contain 25 and 50 problems, respectively). To support reliable evaluation of next-generation frontier models, we introduce Soohak, a 439-problem benchmark newly authored from scratch by 64 mathematicians. Soohak comprises two subsets. On the Challenge subset, frontier models including Gemini-3-Pro, GPT-5, and Claude-Opus-4.5 reach 30.4%, 26.4%, and 10.4% respectively, leaving substantial headroom, while leading open-weight models such as Qwen3-235B, GPT-OSS-120B, and Kimi-2.5 remain below 15%. Notably, beyond standard problem solving, Soohak introduces a refusal subset that probes a capability intrinsic to research mathematics: recognizing ill-posed problems and pausing rather than producing confident but unjustified answers. On this subset, no model exceeds 50%, identifying refusal as a new optimization target that current models do not directly address. To prevent contamination, the dataset will be publicly released in late 2026, with model evaluations available upon request in the interim.

</details>


### [111] [Dynamic Meta-Metrics: Source-Sentence Conditioned Weighting for MT Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09098)
*Luke Zhang,Justin Vasselli,Aditya Khan,York Hay Ng,En-Shiun Annie Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出动态元指标（DMM）框架，根据源句子特性动态组合现有机器翻译评估指标，实验表明MLP组合优于线性模型，软条件化进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器翻译评估指标组合方法通常使用静态集成或语言特定加权，无法根据源语句特性动态调整，导致评估准确性受限。

Method: 提出了动态元指标（DMM）框架，包括硬条件化（为每个聚类拟合可解释的组合器）和软条件化（权重随源聚类责任连续变化），使用MLP、线性模型和高斯过程进行组合。

Result: 在WMT Metrics Shared Task多语言对数据上，MLP组合优于线性和高斯过程集成；软条件化引入带来相对于线性模型的性能提升。

Conclusion: 基于源句子条件化的动态指标组合能有效提升机器翻译评估效果，MLP架构和软条件化机制是关键技术选择。

Abstract: We propose Dynamic Meta-Metrics (DMM), a framework for machine translation evaluation that learns source-sentence conditioned combinations of existing metrics. Rather than relying on a single static ensemble or language-specific weighting, DMM adapts the metric combination based on properties of the source segment. We study hard conditioning, which fits an interpretable combiner per cluster, and an exploratory soft-conditioned extension whose weights vary continuously with source-cluster responsibilities. We evaluate DMM on the WMT Metrics Shared Task data across multiple language pairs using pairwise agreement measures at the system and segment levels. Across settings, MLP-based combinations outperform linear and Gaussian process-based ensembles, and introducing soft conditioning yields gains over linear models.

</details>


### [112] [GRC: Unifying Reasoning-Driven Generation, Retrieval and Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09100)
*Zhongtao Miao,Qiyu Wu,Yoshimasa Tsuruoka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GRC训练框架，通过元潜在标记和统一的生成、表示、压缩调优方法，将推理驱动的生成、推理增强的文本表示和上下文压缩三个任务统一在LLM单次前向传播中。该框架支持模块化推理（LEGO风格），减少RAG部署成本，实现高效推理和三倍数据利用率，并提出了自推理潜在嵌入和潜在记忆增强生成等新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文本嵌入和生成任务通常基于LLM分别训练，导致训练成本高、部署负担大；上下文压缩是一个紧迫挑战，对推理驱动的生成和智能体任务至关重要。现有方法缺乏统一框架，无法在一个前向传播中同时处理推理、嵌入和压缩任务。

Method: 提出GRC框架：(1) 使用元潜在标记作为统一接口；(2) 设计统一的生成、表示和压缩联合调优方法；(3) 提出混合分页注意力加速推理；(4) 创新性地引入自推理潜在嵌入和潜在记忆增强生成范式，其中压缩后的O(1)长度KV缓存作为可更新记忆。

Result: 在推理密集型检索基准、生成任务、文档压缩、延迟评估和RAG设置上的大量实验证明了方法的有效性。实现三倍数据利用率，O(1)长度KV缓存压缩，高效推理性能。

Conclusion: GRC框架为真正统一的、能无缝处理推理驱动生成、嵌入和压缩任务的模型提供了新方向。该方法减少RAG部署成本，开启文本嵌入和生成的新范式，为未来统一模型研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Text embedding and generative tasks are usually trained separately based on large language models (LLMs) nowadays. This causes a large amount of training cost and deployment effort. Context compression is also a challenging and pressing task, which is vital to reasoning-driven generation, and agentic tasks requiring long context and continual learning. In this paper, we explore how to unify reasoning-driven generation, reasoning-enhanced text representation and context compression tasks in one forward pass for LLMs. Through meta latent tokens and a unified generative, representative and compressive tuning approach, we propose a training framework named GRC that bridges the three tasks. The trained models can accomplish three objectives in a single forward pass while maintaining modular, LEGO-style flexibility during inference. This design greatly reduces the deployment effort for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) and achieves efficient inference and three times data utilization during training. Furthermore, this framework design enables a new paradigm for text embedding: self-reason-latent embeds, and a new generation paradigm, latent memory-augmented generation, where compressed and internalized KV cache with O(1) length is used as the updatable memory. We also propose hybrid paged attention to speed up the inference of our models. Extensive experiments on reasoning-intensive retrieval benchmarks, generative tasks, document compression, latency evaluation, and RAG settings demonstrate the effectiveness of our method and may shed light on the truly unified model that can handle reasoning-driven generation, embedding and compression tasks seamlessly.

</details>


### [113] [Fin-Bias: Comprehensive Evaluation for LLM Decision-Making under human bias in Finance Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09106)
*Xiaoyu Hu,Jinman Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Fin-Bias基准，用于评估LLM在金融投资决策中对人类偏见的从众行为，发现LLM倾向于跟随上下文中的显式偏差，并开发了一种检测方法促使模型独立思考，部分模型在预测股票回报上超越人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融基准局限于小样本，未能评估LLM在不确定性和人类偏见语境下的易感性，因此需要构建更大规模、更贴近真实金融场景的基准来检测LLM的可靠性与对齐问题。

Method: 构建包含8868份长文本分析师报告的Fin-Bias基准，涵盖多行业公司分析及投资评级；通过向LLM提供包含/不包含分析师评级、甚至虚假评级的报告，获取LLM生成的投资评级，对比分析其从众行为。

Result: LLM倾向于跟随上下文中的显式偏见（即从众行为）；提出的偏见检测方法可促使LLM独立判断，部分模型在预测未来股票回报方面甚至超越人类分析师。

Conclusion: LLM在金融决策中存在显著的从众偏差，但通过适当的检测与引导方法，可以提升其独立决策能力，甚至达到超越人类的表现。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in financial contexts, raising critical concerns about reliability, alignment, and susceptibility to adversarial manipulation. While prior finance-related benchmarks assess LLMs' capabilities in stock trading, they are often restricted to small sample and fail to demonstrate LLM susceptibility to context with potential human bias. We introduce Fin-Bias (financial herding under long and uncertain financial context), a benchmark for evaluating LLM investment decision-making when faced with uncertainty and possible human-biased opinions. Fin-Bias includes 8868 long firm-specific analyst reports, including firm aspects summarized and analyzed by sophisticated analysts with investment ratings (Bullish/Neutral/Bearish) spanning from various industries. We present large language models with firm analyst reports with/without analyst investment ratings and even with 'fake' rating, to get investment ratings generated by LLMs. Our results reveal that LLMs tend to herd the explicit bias in context. We also develop a method to detect potential human opinions, which can encourage LLMs to think independently, some models even exceed human performance in predicting future stock return.

</details>


### [114] [From Traditional Taggers to LLMs: A Comparative Study of POS Tagging for Medieval Romance Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09147)
*Matthias Schöffel,Esteban Garces Arias*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了LLM在中世纪罗曼语（中世纪奥克语、加泰罗尼亚语、法语）词性标注任务上的表现，发现LLM方法优于传统标注器，跨语言迁移学习对低资源语言尤其有效，双语训练在某些场景下优于多语言配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中世纪罗曼语的词性标注因拼写变异、形态复杂和标注资源稀缺而面临挑战，亟需探索现代神经方法（特别是LLM）在这一历史NLP任务中的适用性。

Method: 在三个中世纪罗曼语变体上，系统比较了传统方法（基于规则、统计）与开源LLM在零样本提示、少样本提示、单语微调、跨语言迁移学习四种设置下的词性标注性能。

Result: LLM方法全面超越传统标注器；微调和多语言训练带来最大提升；跨语言迁移学习对低资源语言显著有效；针对特定目标语言的双语训练可优于更广泛的多语言配置。

Conclusion: 语言邻近性和数据集特征是设计历史NLP迁移策略的关键考量因素，LLM可有效部署于数字人文研究中的中世纪文本处理管线。

Abstract: Part-of-speech (POS) tagging for Medieval Romance languages remains challenging due to orthographic variation, morphological complexity, and limited annotated resources. This paper presents a systematic empirical evaluation of large language models (LLMs) for POS tagging across three medieval varieties: Medieval Occitan, Medieval Catalan, and Medieval French. We compare traditional rule-based and statistical taggers with modern open-source LLMs under zero-shot prompting, few-shot prompting, monolingual fine-tuning, and cross-lingual transfer learning settings.
  Experiments on historically grounded datasets show that LLM-based approaches consistently outperform traditional taggers, with fine-tuning and multilingual training yielding the largest improvements. In particular, cross-lingual transfer learning substantially benefits under-resourced varieties, while targeted bilingual training can outperform broader multilingual configurations for specific target languages. The results highlight the importance of linguistic proximity and dataset characteristics when designing transfer strategies for historical NLP.
  These findings provide empirical insights into the applicability of modern neural methods to medieval text processing and provide practical guidance for deploying LLM-based POS tagging pipelines in digital humanities research. All code, models, and processed datasets are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [115] [Lost in Translation? Exploring the Shift in Grammatical Gender from Latin to Occitan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09156)
*Ahan Chatterjee,Matthias Schöffel,Matthias Aßenmacher,Esteban Garces Arias*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可解释的深度学习框架，研究拉丁语到罗曼语言演化过程中语法性别系统从三分（阳性、阴性、中性）到二分（阳性、阴性）的重组现象，分别在词汇和上下文层面进行分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉丁语到罗曼语言的历时演化涉及语法性别系统的重大重组，但现有研究缺乏可解释的计算工具来分析这一低资源历史语言学现象。

Method: 1) 提出针对低资源历史语言场景的新型分词器，优于传统分词策略；2) 在词汇层面评估形态特征对性别预测的贡献；3) 在上下文层面量化不同词性类别对语法性别预测的贡献，从而刻画词形与其句法上下文之间的性别信息分布。

Result: 传统分词策略在该低资源历史场景下不够稳健，提出的分词器性能更优；成功量化了形态特征和词性类别在词汇和上下文两个层面上的性别预测贡献。

Conclusion: 该可解释框架揭示了拉丁语语法性别信息在词形和句法上下文之间的分布特征，为理解性别系统从三分到二分的历时演化提供了计算层面的洞见。

Abstract: The diachronic evolution from Latin to the Romance languages involved a restructuring of the grammatical gender system from a tripartite configuration (masculine, feminine, neuter) to a bipartite one (masculine, feminine). In this work, we introduce an interpretable deep learning framework to investigate this phenomenon at both lexical and contextual levels. First, we show that conventional tokenization strategies are insufficiently robust for this low-resource historical setting, and that our proposed tokenizer improves performance over these baselines. At the lexical level, we evaluate the contribution of morphological features to gender prediction. At the contextual level, we quantify the contributions of different part-of-speech categories to grammatical gender prediction. Together, these analyses characterize the distribution of gender information between the lemma and its sentential context. We make our codebase, datasets, and results publicly available.

</details>


### [116] [WorldSpeech: A Multilingual Speech Corpus from Around the World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09167)
*Antonis Asonitis,Luca A. Lanzendörfer,Frédéric Berdoz,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了WorldSpeech，一个包含76种语言、65k小时24kHz对齐音频-文本数据的多语言语音语料库，微调现有ASR模型后平均相对词错误率降低63.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高资源语言的自动语音识别（ASR）性能良好，但大多数语言由于公开可用的对齐音频-文本数据有限，识别精度严重下降。

Method: 从议会记录、国际广播和公共领域有声书等多种公开来源收集并构建了包含76种语言、65k小时24kHz对齐音频-文本数据的WorldSpeech语料库。

Result: 在11种类型多样的语言上微调现有ASR模型，平均相对词错误率（WER）降低63.5%。

Conclusion: WorldSpeech通过大规模多语言对齐数据的构建，有效缓解了低资源语言ASR性能不足的问题，显著提升了跨语言语音识别的准确性。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) performs well for high-resource languages with abundant paired audio-transcript data, but its accuracy degrades sharply for most languages due to limited publicly available aligned data. To this end, we introduce WorldSpeech, a 24 kHz multilingual speech corpus comprising 65k hours of aligned audio-transcript data across 76 languages, collected from diverse public sources including parliamentary proceedings, international broadcasts, and public-domain audiobooks. For 37 languages, WorldSpeech provides more than 200 hours of aligned speech, with 28 exceeding 500 hours and 24 surpassing 1k hours. Fine-tuning existing ASR models on WorldSpeech results in an average relative Word-Error-Rate reduction of 63.5% across 11 typologically diverse languages.

</details>


### [117] [Two Ways to De-Bias an LLM-as-a-Judge: A Continuous-Score Comparison of Hierarchical Bayesian Calibration and Neural-ODE Score Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09227)
*Andrea Morandi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了两种用于校准LLM-as-a-judge评分偏差的方法：参数化的贝叶斯层次线性校正和非参数化的神经ODE（FFJORD）流模型。实验表明，方法选择主要取决于可用标注数据量：小样本（100个锚点）下线性校正更优，大样本（1500个锚点）下流模型全面胜出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为自动评分器虽然成本低廉，但存在系统性偏差（如宽严不一、中间压缩、奖励冗长回答等）。后验校准可以在不替换评测模型的前提下修正这些偏差，但不同校准方法在小样本和大样本场景下的表现差异尚未被系统研究。

Method: 在UltraFeedback细粒度评分数据集（1700个配对样本，200个留出）上，对比两种校正器：(1) 参数化的层次贝叶斯线性校正（带每评分不确定性）；(2) 非参数的神经ODE流模型（FFJORD）。从三个维度评估：总体均值恢复、逐项准确率、分布形态匹配。

Result: 两种方法均能将原始+0.71分的均值偏差缩小至±0.08以内。100个锚点时，线性校正的KL散度（0.031 vs 0.058）优于流模型，MAE持平；1500个锚点时，流模型全面领先（MAE 0.320 vs 0.359，Pearson 0.922 vs 0.896，KL 0.026 vs 0.037）。线性校正因无法拟合残差的tanh形非线性在1500锚点时饱和，流模型随数据增加持续改进。

Conclusion: 方法选择实质是数据预算问题：小样本场景推荐线性校正，大样本场景推荐流模型。本文给出了面向生产部署的明确决策规则。

Abstract: [Abridged] Using a Large Language Model (LLM) as an automatic rater (LLM-as-a-judge) is cheap but potentially biased: some judges run lenient, others strict, the middle of the scale gets compressed, and verbose answers may be over-rewarded. A common remedy is post-hoc calibration: leave the cheap judge in place and, on a modest set of paired anchors, fit a transformation from raw judge scores to an estimate of the human rating. We compare two correctors that take opposing views on how this mapping should be modeled: a parametric, small-anchor hierarchical Bayesian linear correction with per-score uncertainty, and a non-parametric Neural-ODE (FFJORD) score-transport flow. Both are run head-to-head on UltraFeedback fine-grained_score (1700 paired examples, 200 held out), with calibration split into three operational sub-questions: population-mean recovery, per-item accuracy, and distributional-shape match.
  The headline result is that the choice between methods is primarily a data-budget question. Both correctors close the raw $+0.71$-point mean offset to within $\pm 0.08$ of the GPT-4 reference, at 100 and at 1500 anchors. Past that, the methods swap roles. With 100 anchors, the linear corrector reconstructs the human-score distribution roughly twice as well by KL divergence (0.031 vs. 0.058) and ties the flow on MAE. With 1500 anchors the flow wins on every metric (MAE 0.320 vs. 0.359, Pearson 0.922 vs. 0.896, KL 0.026 vs. 0.037). The Bayesian linear corrector saturates well below 1500 anchors: residual $\tanh$-shaped non-linearity is, by construction, structure a linear correction cannot fit. The flow keeps improving as labels grow. We translate these findings into an explicit decision rule for production deployments.

</details>


### [118] [Matching Meaning at Scale: Evaluating Semantic Search for 18th-Century Intellectual History through the Case of Locke](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09236)
*Yu Wu,Ananth Mahadevan,Filip Ginter,Michael Mathioudakis,Mikko Tolonen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了语义搜索在18世纪思想史研究中的效果，以洛克著作的接受为例，发现语义搜索能比词汇方法检索到更多隐含接受关系，但仍存在"词汇守门"效应，即检索结果部分受限于表层词汇重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前数字化语料库研究主要依赖词汇文本复用检测，只能捕捉逐字引用，无法识别 paraphrases 和复杂的隐含思想互动，因此需要探索语义搜索方法是否能弥补这一不足。

Method: 基于专家标注的语义分类学（semantic taxonomy），评估现成语义搜索流水线（off-the-shelf semantic search pipeline）在18世纪思想史中的表现，以约翰·洛克的著作接受为案例，与词汇基线方法进行对比。

Result: 语义搜索比词汇基线方法检索到更多隐含接受关系；但存在"词汇守门"效应，即检索结果仍部分受限于表层词汇重叠。

Conclusion: 语义检索在分析大型历史语料库中的思想传播方面具有潜力，但也存在局限性，不能完全替代词汇方法。

Abstract: While digitized corpora have transformed the study of intellectual transmission, current methods rely heavily on lexical text reuse detection, capturing verbatim quotations but fundamentally missing paraphrases and complex implicit engagement. This paper evaluates semantic search in 18th-century intellectual history through the reception of John Locke's foundational work. Using expert annotation grounded in a semantic taxonomy, we examine whether an off-the-shelf semantic search pipeline can surface meaning-level correspondences overlooked by lexical methods. Our results demonstrate that semantic search retrieves substantially more implicit receptions than lexical baselines. However, linguistic diagnostics also reveal a "lexical gatekeeping" effect, where retrieval remains partially constrained by surface vocabulary overlap. These findings highlight both the potential and the limitations of semantic retrieval for analyzing the circulation of ideas in large historical corpora. The data is available at https://github.com/COMHIS/locke-sim-data.

</details>


### [119] [Cornerstones or Stumbling Blocks? Deciphering the Rock Tokens in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09253)
*Yuxuan Jiang,Runchao Li,Shubhashis Roy Dipta,Dawei Li,Zhao Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现On-Policy Distillation中存在一类称为"Rock Tokens"的高损失token，它们占总梯度的大部分但功能上对推理性能贡献微弱，且训练后仍持续存在。跳过这些token可显著提升对齐效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR领域已发现关键token驱动推理提升，但OPD中token级别的理解尚属空白。现有理论认为高损失token应随训练收敛而减少，但实证发现与之相悖，因此需要系统性研究这类持续高损失的token。

Method: 通过实证分析OPD训练中每个token的损失变化模式，识别出训练饱和后仍持续高损失的Rock Tokens；通过梯度分析量化其对总梯度的贡献；通过因果干预实验评估其对推理性能的功能性贡献。

Result: Rock Tokens在输出中占比高达18%，梯度过半但自身不更新，对推理贡献可忽略。优化资源大量浪费在结构性和语篇性残余上，学生模型无法也无需内化这些token。

Conclusion: 通过战略性跳过Rock Tokens可以显著简化对齐过程，挑战了统一token权重的必要性，为大规模模型蒸馏提供了更高效的范式。

Abstract: While recent work in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has shown that a small subset of critical tokens disproportionately drives reasoning gains, an analogous token-level understanding of On-Policy Distillation (OPD) remains largely unexplored. In this work, we investigate high-loss tokens, a token type that--as the most direct signal of student-teacher mismatch under OPD's per-token KL objective--should progressively diminish as training converges according to existing studies; however, our empirical analysis shows otherwise. Even after OPD training reaches apparent saturation, a substantial subset of tokens continues to exhibit persistently high loss; these tokens, which we term Rock Tokens, can account for up to 18\% of the tokens in generated outputs. Our investigation reveals two startling paradoxes. First, despite their high occurrence frequency providing a disproportionately large share of total gradient norms, Rock Tokens themselves remain stagnant throughout training, resisting teacher-driven corrections. Second, through causal intervention, we find that these tokens provide negligible functional contribution to the model's actual reasoning performance. These findings suggest that a vast amount of optimization bandwidth is spent on structural and discourse residuals that the student model cannot or need not internalize. By deconstructing these dynamics, we demonstrate that strategically bypassing these ``stumbling blocks'' can significantly streamline the alignment process, challenging the necessity of uniform token weighting and offering a more efficient paradigm for large-scale model distillation.

</details>


### [120] [Beyond Continuity: Challenges of Context Switching in Multi-Turn Dialogue with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09268)
*Aditya Sinha,Harald Steck,Vito Ostuni,Matteo Rinaldi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多轮对话中LLM对用户主题转换（pivot）与细化（refine）的检测能力，以及从历史轮次中筛选相关上下文的能力。基于多领域真实数据集构建合成基准，评估了10个LLM的零样本表现，发现仅部分推理模型和强指令模型能准确检测主题转换，开放权重模型表现差，所有模型均存在位置偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户在多轮对话中经常细化请求或转向新主题，但LLM经常忽略主题转换并继承不相关的历史上下文，导致响应不准确。现有研究缺乏对LLM在多轮对话中理解主题转换能力的系统性压力测试。

Method: 将问题分解为两个子任务：(1) 检测用户当前轮次是pivot（主题转换）还是refine（细化）；(2) 从历史轮次中筛选相关上下文。基于多领域真实数据集构建合成基准，模拟不同难度的上下文转换。评估10个LLM（包括开放权重、闭源和推理模型）的零样本性能。

Result: 仅部分推理模型和强指令LLM能准确检测主题转换；开放权重LLM表现困难，即使有明确线索也常携带过时上下文；所有模型均存在位置偏差。

Conclusion: 基于实验结果，讨论了提升LLM多轮对话长期鲁棒性的关键启示。

Abstract: Users interacting with Large Language Models (LLMs) in a multi-turn conversation routinely refine their requests or pivot to new topics. LLMs, however, often miss these topic shifts and carry over irrelevant context from previous turns, leading to inaccurate responses. In this paper, we stress-test the multi-turn understanding of LLMs and study the following two sub-tasks: (1) detecting whether the user pivots or refines in the current turn, and (2) shortlisting relevant context from previous turns. To this end, we construct synthetic benchmarks based on real-world datasets from varied domains, as to simulate context shifts of different levels of difficulty. We then evaluate the zero-shot performance of ten LLMs (open-weight, closed-source and reasoning), and demonstrate that only some reasoning and strongly instructed LLMs are accurate in detecting pivots; open-weight LLMs struggle with the task and frequently carry stale context even with explicit cues; and all models suffer from a position bias. Based on the results, we discuss key takeaways for improving long-term robustness in multi-turn capabilities for LLMs.

</details>


### [121] [DeltaRubric: Generative Multimodal Reward Modeling via Joint Planning and Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09269)
*Rui Liu,Dian Yu,Zhenwen Liang,Yucheng Shi,Tong Zheng,Runpeng Dai,Haitao Mi,Pratap Tokekar,Leoweiliang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DeltaRubric 将多模态偏好评估重构为"规划-执行"过程：首先生成实例特定的验证检查清单，然后执行这些检查以得出 grounded 判断，并通过多角色强化学习联合优化规划和验证能力，在 VL-RewardBench 上显著提升 Qwen3-VL 4B (+22.6) 和 8B (+18.8) 的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单步评估器存在"懒惰评判"问题——利用语言先验而非细粒度视觉验证。基于 rubrics 的评估在纯文本场景有效，但扩展到多模态时受限于视觉推理的复杂性，因为响应间的关键差异往往依赖实例特定的视觉细节。

Method: DeltaRubric 将多模态偏好评估分解为两步：(1) 分歧规划器 (Disagreement Planner) 生成中立的实例特定验证清单；(2) 清单验证器 (Checklist Verifier) 根据图像和问题执行这些自查，得出 grounded 判断。整个过程通过多角色强化学习联合优化。

Result: 在 Qwen3-VL 4B 和 8B Instruct 模型上验证，DeltaRubric 在 VL-RewardBench 上相比基线模型分别提升 +22.6 和 +18.8 个百分点的准确率，大幅超越无 rubrics 的标准基线。

Conclusion: 将评估分解为结构化、可验证的步骤能够实现更可靠和更具泛化性的多模态奖励建模。

Abstract: Aligning Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) requires reliable reward models, yet existing single-step evaluators can suffer from lazy judging, exploiting language priors over fine-grained visual verification. While rubric-based evaluation mitigates these biases in text-only settings, extending it to multimodal tasks is bottlenecked by the complexity of visual reasoning. The critical differences between responses often depend on instance-specific visual details. Robust evaluation requires dynamically synthesizing rubrics that isolate spatial and factual discrepancies. To address this, we introduce $\textbf{DeltaRubric}$, an approach that reformulates multimodal preference evaluation as a plan-and-execute process within a single MLLM. DeltaRubric operates in two steps: acting first as a $\textit{Disagreement Planner}$, the model generates a neutral, instance-specific verification checklist. Transitioning into a $\textit{Checklist Verifier}$, it executes these self-generated checks against the image and question to produce the final grounded judgment. We formulate DeltaRubric as a multi-role reinforcement learning problem, jointly optimizing planning and verification capabilities. Validated on Qwen3-VL 4B and 8B Instruct models, DeltaRubric achieves solid empirical gains. For instance, On VL-RewardBench, it improves base model overall accuracy by $\textbf{+22.6}$ (4B) and $\textbf{+18.8}$ (8B) points, largely outperforming standard no-rubric baselines. The results demonstrate that decomposing evaluation into structured, verifiable steps leads to more reliable and generalizable multimodal reward modeling.

</details>


### [122] [BetaEdit: Null-Space Constrained Sequential Model Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09285)
*Bingqing Liu,Wei Liu,Yuhua Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对基于零空间方法的模型编辑中存在的知识泄露和长序列编辑性能退化问题，分析了历史感知更新策略的有效性原因，并提出了BetaEdit框架，通过控制知识泄露并融合历史感知更新，在大型语言模型的大规模序列编辑任务中取得了显著优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零空间方法在模型编辑中依赖近似零空间导致知识泄露，且连续编辑时性能严重下降；历史感知策略虽能缓解此问题但原因不明。本文旨在揭示知识泄露的本质并解释历史感知更新的作用机制，进而提出更优的编辑框架。

Method: 首先理论分析现有零空间方法中的知识泄露问题，并解释历史感知更新策略为何能同时保持编辑性能和通用能力；在此基础上提出BetaEdit框架，有效控制知识泄露并将历史感知更新融入零空间范式。

Result: 在三个大型语言模型和两个标准基准上的大量实验表明，BetaEdit在大规模序列编辑这一挑战性场景下一致性地优于先前方法。

Conclusion: BetaEdit通过控制知识泄露并整合历史感知更新，显著提升了零空间模型编辑方法在长序列编辑任务中的表现，为大规模持续模型编辑提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Null-space-based methods have garnered considerable attention in model editing by constraining updates to the null space of the pre-existing knowledge representation, thereby preserving the model's original behavior. However, in practice these methods rely on an approximate null space--leading to knowledge leakage--and further suffer from severe performance degradation during sequential editing. Recent work shows that history-aware editing strategies can empirically mitigate this decline, yet the underlying reason remains unclear. In this paper, we first expose the knowledge leakage inherent in existing null-space approaches and then analyze why history-aware updates effectively preserve both editing performance and general capabilities during long-horizon editing. Building on these insights, we propose BetaEdit, a refined framework that effectively controls the knowledge leakage and integrates history-aware updates into the null-space paradigm. Extensive experiments on three large language models across two standard benchmarks show that BetaEdit consistently outperforms prior methods in the challenging regime of massive-scale sequential editing. Code is available at: https://github.com/lbq8942/BetaEdit.

</details>


### [123] [Test-Time Speculation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09329)
*Avinash Kumar,Sujay Sanghavi,Poulami Das*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对推测解码中接受长度随生成长度退化的问题，提出测试时推测（TTS）方法，通过在线蒸馏持续适配草稿模型，在不增加额外成本的情况下，将接受长度平均提升41%，最高达72%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码器（如DFlash、EAGLE-3、PARD）的接受长度会随输出长度增加而急剧退化，在数千token后接近1（无加速），导致长响应任务中推测解码失效。根本原因在于草稿模型在短序列上离线训练，推理时被迫匹配远超训练分布的长序列输出。

Method: 提出测试时推测（TTS），一种在线蒸馏方法：在测试时持续适应草稿模型。关键洞察是token验证步骤已调用目标模型处理每个草稿token，可提供训练信号而不增加额外成本。将草稿视为学生、目标视为教师，在多个推测轮次中调整草稿，每次更新随生成进行提高草稿准确性。

Result: 在Qwen-3、Qwen-3.5和Llama3.1系列模型上，TTS相比最先进的推测解码器，接受长度平均提升41%，最高达72%，且收益随生成长度增加而扩大。

Conclusion: TTS有效解决了推测解码在长序列生成中的性能退化问题，通过利用验证阶段已有的计算资源进行在线蒸馏，实现了无需额外开销的持续适配，为长响应LLM推理加速提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by using a fast draft model to generate tokens and a more accurate target model to verify them. Its performance depends on the $\textit{acceptance length}$, or number of draft tokens accepted by the target. Our studies show that the acceptance length of even state-of-the-art speculators, like DFlash, EAGLE-3 and PARD degrade with generation length, reaching values close to 1 (i.e. no speedup) within just a few thousand output tokens, making speculators ineffective for long-response tasks. Acceptance lengths decline because most speculators are trained offline on short sequences, but are forced to match the target model on much longer outputs at inference, well beyond their training distribution.
  To address this issue, we propose $\textit{Test-Time Speculation (TTS)}$, an online distillation approach that continuously adapts the speculator at test-time. TTS leverages the key insight that the token verification step already invokes the target model for each draft token, providing the training signal needed to adapt the draft at no additional cost. Treating the draft as the student and the target as a teacher, TTS adjusts the draft over several speculation rounds, with each update improving the draft's accuracy as generation proceeds. Our results across multiple models from the Qwen-3, Qwen-3.5, and Llama3.1 families show that TTS improves acceptance lengths over state-of-the-art speculators by up to $72\%$ and $41\%$ on average, with the benefits scaling with increased generation lengths.

</details>


### [124] [RuPLaR : Efficient Latent Compression of LLM Reasoning Chains with Rule-Based Priors From Multi-Step to One-Step](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09346)
*Xiaocheng Luo,Kang Wang,Zaifu Zhan,Yuechi Zhou,Xiangyu Duan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RuPLaR框架，通过"One-Model One-Step"的方式将潜在思维链推理压缩为单模型单阶段训练，利用基于规则的先验概率分布指导潜在推理token生成，以联合训练目标（交叉熵+KL散度+语义对齐）实现高效推理，在提升准确率11.1%的同时大幅降低token使用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT方法受限于自然语言的低效性和表达能力限制，而潜在CoT方法虽在连续潜在空间中推理，但多步骤或多模型范式存在结构复杂、误差传播和协调开销大等问题，亟需一种简洁高效的潜在推理压缩框架。

Method: 提出RuPLaR框架：1）单模型单阶段训练，让LLM自主生成潜在推理token；2）基于规则的先验概率分布指导推理过程；3）联合训练目标包含三项损失：交叉熵保证答案一致性、KL散度实现Soft Thinking约束对齐软token与规则先验、表征空间中的问题-推理语义对齐约束。

Result: 相比于现有潜在CoT方法，RuPLaR在准确率上提升11.1%，且使用最少的token数量，验证了其有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: RuPLaR通过消除级联过程和跨模型依赖，以简洁的单模型单阶段方式实现了高效潜在推理，为LLM推理提供了一种更优的压缩范式。

Abstract: The Chain-of-Thought (CoT) paradigm, while enhancing the interpretability of Large Language Models (LLMs), is constrained by the inefficiencies and expressive limits of natural language. Latent Chain-of-Thought (latent CoT) reasoning, which operates in a continuous latent space, offers a promising alternative but faces challenges from structural complexities in existing multi-step or multi-model paradigms, such as error propagation and coordination overhead. In this paper, we introduce One-Model One-Step, a novel compression framework for Latent Reasoning with Rule-Based Priors(RuPLaR) to address this challenge. Our method trains an LLM to autonomously generate latent reasoning tokens in a single training stage, guided by rule-based prior probability distributions, thereby eliminating cascaded processes and inter-model dependencies. To ensure reasoning quality, we design a joint training objective that enforces answer consistency via cross-entropy, aligns soft tokens with rule-based priors via KL divergence (the Soft Thinking constraint), and adds a problem-thought semantic alignment constraint in the representation space. Extensive experiments show that our compression framework not only improves accuracy by 11.1% over existing latent CoT methods but also achieves this with minimal token usage, underscoring its effectiveness and extensibility. Code: https://github.com/xiaocen-luo/RuPLaR.

</details>


### [125] [HOME-KGQA: A Benchmark Dataset for Multimodal Knowledge Graph Question Answering on Household Daily Activities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09348)
*Shusaku Egami,Aoi Ohta,Tomoki Tsujimura,Masaki Asada,Tatsuya Ishigaki,Ken Fukuda,Masahiro Hamasaki,Hiroya Takamura*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了HOME-KGQA，一个基于日常家庭活动多模态知识图谱的KGQA基准数据集，包含复杂多跳自然语言问题，涉及多级时空推理、多模态对齐和聚合函数。实验表明，现有基于LLM的KGQA方法在该数据集上表现不佳，揭示了面向真实世界部署的KGQA系统面临的重大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGQA基准数据集存在三个主要局限：偏向百科全书式知识、局限于单一模态、缺乏细粒度时空数据。这使得它们难以适用于具身AI所面向的真实世界场景。因此，需要构建一个更贴近实际应用、包含多模态和复杂时空推理的KGQA基准。

Method: 构建了基于日常家庭活动多模态知识图谱的HOME-KGQA基准数据集，包含复杂多跳自然语言问题及其对应的图数据库查询语言。问题设计涵盖多级时空推理、多模态对齐和聚合函数等挑战性任务。

Result: 在HOME-KGQA上评估了多种基于LLM的KGQA方法，结果表明这些方法未能达到与现有数据集相当的性能水平，凸显了现有方法在复杂多模态时空推理任务上的不足。

Conclusion: HOME-KGQA揭示了当前KGQA方法在真实部署场景中的局限性，特别是在多级时空推理、多模态对齐和聚合查询方面。该数据集为未来研究提供了重要基准，推动KGQA系统向更贴近实际应用的方向发展。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) provide flexible natural language processing capabilities, while knowledge graphs (KGs) offer explicit and structured knowledge. Integrating these two in a complementary manner enables the development of reliable and verifiable AI systems. In particular, knowledge graph question answering (KGQA) has attracted attention as a means to reduce LLM hallucinations and to leverage knowledge beyond the training data. However, existing KGQA benchmark datasets are biased toward encyclopedic knowledge, limited to a single modality, and lack fine-grained spatiotemporal data, which limits their applicability to real-world scenarios targeted by Embodied AI. We introduce HOME-KGQA, a novel KGQA benchmark dataset built on a multimodal KG of daily household activities. HOME-KGQA consists of complex, multi-hop natural language questions paired with graph database query languages. Compared to existing benchmarks, it includes more challenging questions that involve multi-level spatiotemporal reasoning, multimodal grounding, and aggregate functions. Experimental results show that the LLM-based KGQA methods fail to achieve performance comparable to that on existing datasets when evaluated on HOME-KGQA. This highlights significant challenges that should be addressed for the real-world deployment of KGQA systems. Our dataset is available at https://github.com/aistairc/home-kgqa

</details>


### [126] [Cross-Cultural Transfer of Emoji Semantics and Sentiment in Financial Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09414)
*Ahmed Mahrous,Roberto Di Pietro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了表情符号在不同语言、平台和资产社区中的情感信号可迁移性，发现表情符号的语义和情感极性具有跨社区稳定性，且包含表情符号的模型能显著缩小情感迁移差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表情符号在在线金融交流中被广泛使用，但其在不同语言、平台和资产社区之间是否提供可迁移的情感信号尚不清楚，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 使用四种语言（Twitter和StockTwits）的大规模语料，测量跨社区的表情符号使用频率、语义和情感极性的差异，并评估在表情符号仅输入、文本仅输入和文本+表情符号输入条件下训练的情感模型。

Result: 表情符号频率在不同社区（尤其是跨语言）存在差异，但语义和情感极性基本稳定；跨资产迁移性能下降最小，跨语言迁移最具挑战性；包含表情符号的模型一致地减少了相对于纯文本模型的迁移差距。

Conclusion: 金融交流中存在部分共享的"表情符号代码"，表情符号提供了紧凑、语言无关的情感线索，能够提升模型在不同市场和平台上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Emojis are widely used in online financial communication, but it is unclear whether they provide transferable sentiment signals across languages, platforms, and asset communities. This study examines the extent to which emoji usage, semantics, and sentiment polarity remain stable across financial communities, and how these layers influence zero-shot sentiment transfer. Using large corpora of Twitter and StockTwits posts in four languages, we measure cross-community divergence and evaluate sentiment models trained under emoji-only, text-only, and text+emoji inputs.
  We find that emoji frequencies differ across communities, especially across languages, but their semantics and sentiment polarity are largely stable. Cross-asset transferability shows minimal degradation, while cross-language transfer remains the most challenging. Including emojis consistently reduces transfer gaps relative to text-only models. These results indicate that financial communication exhibits a partially shared ``emoji code,'' and that emojis provide compact, language-independent sentiment cues that improve model generalization across markets and platforms.

</details>


### [127] [Perception Without Engagement: Dissecting the Causal Discovery Deficit in LMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09422)
*Jiafeng Liang,Zhihao Zhu,Zihan Zhang,Baoqi Ren,Shixin Jiang,Runxuan Liu,Tao Ren,Ming Liu,See-Kiong Ng,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ProCauEval评估协议，通过五种扰动配置系统分解视觉和文本模态对因果发现的贡献，发现LMMs虽然能感知视频内容但在因果推理中系统性地未充分利用视觉信息。在此基础上提出Anti-Distillation Policy Optimization (ADPO)强化学习框架，通过负教师对齐迫使模型基于视觉证据而非文本捷径进行推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LMMs在视频理解中易受文本先验捷径影响，但已有基准仅测量响应准确性，无法揭示缺陷的根源和程度。需要从结果评估转向机制诊断，系统解耦视觉和文本模态的贡献，并开发相应缓解方法。

Method: 提出ProCauEval扰动评估协议，包含五种控制配置系统操纵视觉和文本模态；提出ADPO强化学习框架，基于负教师对齐，通过最大化原始输入与视觉损坏输入之间的策略分布散度，迫使模型将推理基于视觉证据而非文本捷径。

Result: 评估17个主流LMMs发现：模型能忠实感知视频内容但在因果推理中系统性地未充分利用视觉信息；更强的后训练会放大而非缓解文本先验依赖；更高基线性能与更大的扰动脆弱性相关。ADPO能提升视觉参与度而不牺牲基础理解能力。

Conclusion: ProCauEval揭示了LMMs在因果发现中的机制性缺陷，ADPO为缓解文本先验捷径提供了有效且初步的解决方案，推动了可靠因果发现的研究方向。

Abstract: Although Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have achieved strong performance on general video understanding, their susceptibility to textual prior shortcuts during causal discovery has been recognized as a critical deficit. The underlying mechanisms of this phenomenon remain incompletely understood, as existing benchmarks only measure response accuracy without revealing the sources and extent of the deficit. We introduce ProCauEval, a perturbation-based evaluation protocol that shifts from outcome assessment to mechanism diagnosis, probing causal discovery through five controlled configurations that systematically manipulate visual and textual modalities to decompose their respective contributions to model behavior and dissect the failure modes. Evaluating 17 mainstream LMMs, we find that models faithfully perceive video content yet systematically underexploit it during causal reasoning. We further observe that stronger post-training amplifies rather than mitigates textual prior reliance, and that higher baseline performance correlates with greater fragility under perturbation. To address these, we propose Anti-Distillation Policy Optimization (ADPO), a reinforcement learning framework built on negative teacher alignment, which augments GRPO by explicitly pushing the policy away from a prior-only counterfactual teacher induced by visual corruption. Specifically, ADPO maximizes the divergence between the policy distributions conditioned on the original and visually corrupted inputs, thereby forcing the model to ground its reasoning in visual evidence rather than textual shortcuts. Extensive experiments show that ADPO improves visual engagement without sacrificing fundamental comprehension, thus offering a preliminary step toward reliable causal discovery.

</details>


### [128] [PumpSense: Real-Time Detection and Target Extraction of Crypto Pump-and-Dumps on Telegram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09431)
*Ahmed Mahrous,Roberto Di Pietro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了超过28万条Telegram帖子的加密货币拉高出货检测语料库，提出实时泵检测（LightGBM F1=0.79/延迟9.4s；BGE-M3 F1=0.83/延迟50ms）和靶标提取任务，证明基于消息分析可在微秒级检测泵事件，LLM在靶标提取上达到0.91准确率，优于传统规则方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加密货币拉高出货检测研究缺乏消息级标注数据，导致无法兼顾可靠性与实时性；同时传统规则方法因代币歧义难以准确提取靶标加密货币和交易所，亟需新的解决方案。

Method: 1. 构建包含280,000+条Telegram帖子的语料库（来自39个组织群组），人工标注2,246个泵公告及其靶标；2. 定义实时泵公告检测任务，对比LightGBM（树模型）与BGE-M3（Transformer）两种方案；3. 定义靶标加密货币/交易所提取任务，对比规则方法与LLM方法。

Result: 检测任务：LightGBM F1=0.79（延迟9.4秒/样本），BGE-M3 F1=0.83（延迟50毫秒/样本）；提取任务：传统规则方法效果差，LLM取得最高准确率0.91；推理速度可达微秒级/窗口。

Conclusion: 基于消息分析的泵检测方法可在单个消息窗口实现近即时检测，相较于依赖市场数据的方法（需等待异常交易出现后数十秒）具有显著优势；首个建立靶标提取基准，证实LLM是解决代币歧义问题的有效方案。

Abstract: Cryptocurrency pump-and-dump schemes coordinated via Telegram threaten market integrity. However, existing research addressing this specific threat has not yet produced solutions that combine reliable results with fast response. This is in part due to the absence of publicly available, message-level labeled data, as well as design choices. In this paper, we address both issues. In particular, we introduce a corpus of over 280,000 Telegram posts from 39 pump-organizing groups, all manually reviewed to identify 2,246 pump announcements and their targeted cryptocurrency and exchange. Leveraging this dataset, we define two tasks: real-time pump-announcement detection and target cryptocurrency/exchange extraction. For detection, we compare two machine-learning models: a lightweight tree-based LightGBM classifier (F1=0.79, latency=9.4 s/sample) and a transformer-based BGE-M3 (F1=0.83, latency=50 ms/sample). With our proposed approach, we show that message analysis can achieve near-instant pump detection at the level of individual Telegram message windows. Unlike prior work that relies purely on market data and typically detects pumps tens of seconds after abnormal trading activity is observed, our method operates directly on the coordination messages themselves and can be evaluated in microseconds per window on commodity hardware. To our knowledge, we also establish the first benchmark for manipulated coin and exchange extraction. We demonstrate that traditional rule-based extraction methods, widely relied upon in prior literature, are ineffective due to ticker ambiguity. In contrast, LLMs achieve the highest accuracy with a score of 0.91.

</details>


### [129] [Key Coverage Matters: Semi-Structured Extraction of OCR Clinical Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09440)
*Yu Wang,Yingyun Li,Ying Qin,Haiyang Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对临床报告因隐私法规和数据孤岛导致信息碎片化的问题，本文提出基于关键字段条件的抽取式问答方法，通过构建规范键库实现OCR临床报告的结构化提取。在20余家医院的真实报告上，0.2B BERT模型在Top-90规范键覆盖下达到0.839（精确匹配）和0.893（边界容忍匹配）的F1分数，表明键覆盖率是端到端性能的主导因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床报告因隐私法规和数据孤岛而分散在不同医疗机构，患者转诊时只能携带纸质或扫描报告，阻碍了电子健康记录整合、纵向回顾、患者管理、随访、真实世界研究和临床试验匹配等下游应用。虽然OCR可以数字化这些报告，但由于临床文档异质性高、OCR文本噪声大、许多医疗机构需要低成本的本地部署，可靠提取仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 将问题形式化为基于规范键条件的抽取式问答（key-conditioned extractive question answering）任务，处理来源于OCR的临床报告。由于关键字段既非固定也非预先已知，键空间是开放的。通过迭代的键挖掘、归一化、聚类和轻量级人工验证来维护一个规范键库（canonical key inventory），并引入键覆盖率（key coverage）作为量化库完整性的指标。使用0.2B参数的BERT模型进行实验。

Result: 在20余家医院的真实报告上，模型性能随着键覆盖率单调提升。在Top-90规范键覆盖下，精确匹配F1为0.839，边界容忍匹配F1为0.893。Top-90覆盖下，该模型在精确匹配上优于微调的Qwen3-0.6B基线。键覆盖率是端到端性能的主导因素。

Conclusion: 本文方法依赖于半结构化临床报告中与语言无关的键值组织结构，具有跨语言和跨场景的适应性。给定适当的规范键库和别名映射，该方法可适应其他语言和设置。键覆盖率是影响系统性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Clinical reports are often fragmented across healthcare institutions because privacy regulations and data silos limit direct information sharing. When patients seek care at a different hospital, they often carry paper or scanned reports from prior visits. This hinders EHR integration and longitudinal review, and downstream applications that depend on more complete patient records, such as patient management, follow-up care, real-world studies, and clinical-trial matching. Although OCR can digitize such reports, reliable extraction remains challenging because clinical documents are heterogeneous, OCR text is noisy, and many healthcare settings require low-cost on-premise deployment. We formulate this problem as canonical key-conditioned extractive question answering over OCR-derived clinical reports. Because the key fields are neither fixed nor known in advance, the key space is open. We maintain a canonical key inventory through iterative key mining, normalization, clustering, and lightweight human verification, and introduce key coverage as a metric to quantify inventory completeness. Using a 0.2B BERT-based model, experiments on real-world reports from more than 20 hospitals show performance improves monotonically with key coverage. The model achieves F1 scores of 0.839 and 0.893 under exact match and boundary-tolerant matching, respectively, once the Top-90 canonical keys are covered. These results show that key coverage is a dominant factor for end-to-end performance. At Top-90 coverage, our model outperforms a fine-tuned Qwen3-0.6B baseline under exact match. Although our annotated corpus is Chinese, the method relies on the language-agnostic key-value organization of semi-structured clinical reports and can be adapted to other settings given an appropriate canonical key inventory and alias mapping.

</details>


### [130] [Beyond Position Bias: Shifting Context Compression from Position-Driven to Semantic-Driven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09463)
*Jiwei Tang,Zhijing Huang,Xinyu Zhang,Chen Jason Zhang,Jianxing Yu,Libin Zheng,Rui Meng,Jian Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SeCo是一种从位置驱动转向语义驱动的上下文压缩方法，通过选择查询相关token作为语义中心并进行一致性加权聚合，有效消除位置偏差，在14个基准测试上展现出优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在长上下文场景中存在高计算开销和信息冗余问题，而当前的软提示压缩方法受限于位置偏差，依赖固定位置的token插入或根据物理布局分组，导致性能不稳定和语义碎片化。

Method: SeCo提出语义驱动的上下文压缩范式：动态选择与查询相关的token作为语义中心，通过一致性加权合并对其余token进行聚合，从而在语义空间中直接完成压缩，摆脱物理token布局的约束。

Result: 在14个基准测试、两种骨干模型上的实验表明，SeCo在下游任务性能、推理延迟和域外鲁棒性方面均表现出优越性。

Conclusion: SeCo通过语义一致性上下文压缩有效克服了位置偏差问题，为长上下文LLM部署提供了一种高效且鲁棒的压缩方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated exceptional performance across diverse tasks. However, their deployment in long-context scenarios faces high computational overhead and information redundancy. While soft prompt compression has emerged as a promising way to mitigate these costs by compressing sequences into compact embeddings, existing paradigms remain fundamentally constrained by position bias: they primarily rely on learnable tokens insertion at fixed positions or group tokens according to their physical token layout, thereby inducing performance instability and semantic fragmentation. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose Semantic Consistency Context Compression (SeCo), a method that shifts context compression from position-driven to semantic-driven. Rather than constraint by physical token layout, SeCo dynamically anchors compression directly in the semantic space by selecting query-relevant tokens as semantic centers and aggregating remaining tokens via consistency-weighted merging. This design inherently preserves semantic consistency while eliminating position bias. Extensive experiments on 14 benchmarks across two backbone models demonstrate that SeCo consistently shows superiority in downstream tasks, inference latency, and out-of-domain robustness. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/seco-EE5E.

</details>


### [131] [Align and Shine: Building High-Quality Sentence-Aligned Corpora for Multilingual Text Simplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09476)
*Kenji Hilasaca,Nouran Khallaf,Serge Sharoff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过众包方式从可比语料库中收集并处理了多语言（加泰罗尼亚语、英语、法语、意大利语和西班牙语）的文本简化数据，构建了可用于训练和测试文本简化系统的句子级对齐数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本简化对提升信息可读性至关重要，但非英语语言缺乏大规模、高质量的简化数据集，限制了多语言文本简化系统的开发。

Method: 采用众包方式从可比语料库中收集简化数据，并设计了从文档级数据中实现句子级对齐的机制，覆盖五种语言。

Result: 构建了一个多语言（加泰罗尼亚语、英语、法语、意大利语和西班牙语）的句子级对齐简化语料库，并公开可用。

Conclusion: 该数据集填补了非英语语言文本简化资源的空白，为多语言文本简化系统的训练和评估提供了基础。

Abstract: Text simplification plays a crucial role in improving the accessibility and comprehensibility of written information for diverse audiences, including language learners and readers with limited literacy. Despite its importance, large-scale, high-quality datasets for training and evaluating text simplification models remain scarce for languages other than English. This paper reports an experimental study on the collection and processing of crowd-sourced simplification data from comparable corpora to construct a corpus suitable for both training and testing text simplification systems across multiple languages (Catalan, English, French, Italian and Spanish). We report mechanisms for sentence-level alignment from document-level data. The resulting dataset of the aligned sentence pairs is publicly available.

</details>


### [132] [A Cognitively Grounded Bayesian Framework for Misinformation Susceptibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09483)
*Pranava Madhyastha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Bounded Pragmatic Listener (BPL)框架，通过扩展理性言语行为理论并结合三个认知约束（递归深度限制、先验压缩参数、可用性样本量），建模 misinformation 的易感性，并在LIAR和MultiFC基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 misinformation 检测方法缺乏对认知机制的建模，无法解释个体间对错误信息的易感性差异、标注者分歧以及不同信息失调类型（mis-, dis-, mal-information）的差异性脆弱性。

Method: 在理性言语行为（RSA）理论基础上引入三个认知约束：a) 递归深度限制（建模工作记忆上限）；b) 先验压缩参数（建模信息瓶颈）；c) 可用性样本量（以显著性加权的提议实现重要性采样）。

Result: 在LIAR和MultiFC基准上实现了具有竞争力的真实性分类性能，并为深度不匹配悖论提供了实验支持。

Conclusion: BPL框架能够有效建模 misinformation 易感性的认知基础，为理解标注者分歧和信息失调类型的差异性提供了理论支持。

Abstract: In this (work in progress) paper, we present Bounded Pragmatic Listener (or BPL), a cognitively grounded Bayesian framework for modelling susceptibility to information disorder. BPL extends Rational Speech Act theory with three cognitively motivated bounds derived from the bounded rationality literature with a) a recursion depth bound (that emphasises working memory limits);b) a prior compression parameter (which is oriented at capturing information bottleneck); and c) an availability sample size (that operationalises importance sampling with saliency-weighted proposals). This allows us to test predictions about misinformation susceptibility, annotator disagreement, and the differential vulnerability to mis-, dis-, and mal-information as defined in the Information Disorder framework. We validate BPL on the LIAR and MultiFC benchmarks showcasing competitive veracity classification and experimental support for the depth-mismatch paradox.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Where Reliability Lives in Vision-Language Models: A Mechanistic Study of Attention, Hidden States, and Causal Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08200)
*Logan Mann,Ajit Saravanan,Ishan Dave,Shikhar Shiromani,Saadullah Ismail,Yi Xia,Emily Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统性地检验了视觉-语言模型（VLM）中一个普遍直觉——注意力图越尖锐、集中在查询区域，模型回答越可信。通过开发统一机制分析工具VRP，对LLaVA-1.5、PaliGemma、Qwen2-VL三个开源VLM族进行研究，发现注意力结构几乎无法预测回答正确性（R_pb≈0.001），而隐状态几何和后层稀疏电路才是可信度的可靠信号。同时揭示了不同架构（晚融合vs早融合）在可靠性编码方式上的根本差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验VLM中一个广泛但未经严格验证的直觉假设：注意力图的锐度（集中在查询区域的程度）与模型回答的可信度正相关。

Method: 开发VLM可靠性探测（VRP）统一机制分析流程，比较注意力结构、生成动态和隐状态几何与正确性标签的关系。在3个VLM族（LLaVA-1.5、PaliGemma、Qwen2-VL，3-7B参数）上进行实验，包括因果消融、线性探测、自一致性分析等。

Result: (1)注意力结构对正确性预测力接近零（R_pb=0.001），但注意力的因果必要性依然显著（前30%patch掩码导致准确率下降8.2-11.3pp）；(2)可靠性在计算后期变得可读，单层隐状态线性探测AUROC>0.95，K=10自一致性是最强行为预测因子(R_pb=0.43)；(3)因果消融揭示架构差异：晚融合LLaVA将可靠性集中在脆弱的后瓶颈层（消融前5神经元导致准确率下降8.3pp），早融合模型则分布广泛且鲁棒。

Conclusion: 在3-7B VLM中，可靠性的可靠读取信号来自隐状态几何、逐层边际形成和后层稀疏电路，而非注意力图锐度。这一发现对设计可信度监控器具有直接的架构指导意义。

Abstract: A pervasive intuition holds that vision-language models (VLMs) are most trustworthy when their attention maps look sharp: concentrated attention on the queried region should imply a confident, calibrated answer. We test this Attention-Confidence Assumption directly. We instrument three open-weight VLM families (LLaVA-1.5, PaliGemma, Qwen2-VL; 3-7B parameters) with a unified mechanistic pipeline -- the VLM Reliability Probe (VRP) -- that compares attention structure, generation dynamics, and hidden-state geometry against a single correctness label. Three results emerge. (i) Attention structure is a near-zero predictor of correctness (R_pb(C_k,y)=0.001, 95% CI [-0.034,0.036]; R_pb(H_s,y)=-0.012, [-0.047,0.024] on a pooled n=3,090 split), even though attention remains causally necessary for feature extraction (top-30% patch masking drops accuracy by 8.2-11.3 pp, p<0.001). (ii) Reliability becomes legible later in the computation: a single hidden-state linear probe reaches AUROC>0.95 on POPE for two of three families, and self-consistency at K=10 is the strongest behavioral predictor we measure at 10x inference cost (R_pb=0.43). (iii) Causal neuron-level ablations expose a sharp architectural split with direct monitor-design implications: late-fusion LLaVA concentrates reliability in a fragile late bottleneck (-8.3 pp object-identification accuracy after top-5 probe-neuron ablation), whereas early-fusion PaliGemma and Qwen2-VL distribute it widely and absorb destruction of ~50% of their peak-layer hidden dimension with <=1 pp degradation. The takeaway is narrow but consequential: in 3-7B VLMs, reliability is read more reliably off hidden-state geometry, layer-wise margin formation, and sparse late-layer circuits than off attention-map sharpness.

</details>


### [134] [Auto-Rubric as Reward: From Implicit Preferences to Explicit Multimodal Generative Criteria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08354)
*Juanxi Tian,Fengyuan Liu,Jiaming Han,Yilei Jiang,Yongliang Wu,Yesheng Liu,Haodong Li,Furong Xu,Wanhua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Auto-Rubric as Reward (ARR)框架，将隐式偏好知识显式化为可解释的评分标准，结合Rubric Policy Optimization (RPO)实现结构化多维度奖励建模，在文本到图像生成和图像编辑任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF方法将人类偏好的多维度结构压缩为标量或成对标签，易受奖励黑客攻击；而Rubrics-as-Reward方法在生成可靠、可扩展且数据高效的评分标准上仍面临挑战。

Method: ARR通过外部化VLM的隐式偏好知识为提示特定的评分标准，将整体意图分解为独立可验证的质量维度；RPO将ARR的结构化多维度评估蒸馏为稳健的二元奖励，替代不透明的标量回归。

Result: 在文本到图像生成和图像编辑基准上，ARR-RPO优于成对奖励模型和VLM评判器，显著抑制了位置偏差等评估偏差。

Conclusion: 显式外部化隐式偏好知识为结构化评分标准可以实现更可靠、数据高效的多模态对齐，瓶颈在于缺乏因子化接口而非知识本身。

Abstract: Aligning multimodal generative models with human preferences demands reward signals that respect the compositional, multi-dimensional structure of human judgment. Prevailing RLHF approaches reduce this structure to scalar or pairwise labels, collapsing nuanced preferences into opaque parametric proxies and exposing vulnerabilities to reward hacking. While recent Rubrics-as-Reward (RaR) methods attempt to recover this structure through explicit criteria, generating rubrics that are simultaneously reliable, scalable, and data-efficient remains an open problem. We introduce Auto-Rubric as Reward (ARR), a framework that reframes reward modeling from implicit weight optimization to explicit, criteria-based decomposition. Before any pairwise comparison, ARR externalizes a VLM's internalized preference knowledge as prompt-specific rubrics, translating holistic intent into independently verifiable quality dimensions. This conversion of implicit preference structure into inspectable, interpretable constraints substantially suppresses evaluation biases including positional bias, enabling both zero-shot deployment and few-shot conditioning on minimal supervision. To extend these gains into generative training, we propose Rubric Policy Optimization (RPO), which distills ARR's structured multi-dimensional evaluation into a robust binary reward, replacing opaque scalar regression with rubric-conditioned preference decisions that stabilize policy gradients. On text-to-image generation and image editing benchmarks, ARR-RPO outperforms pairwise reward models and VLM judges, demonstrating that explicitly externalizing implicit preference knowledge into structured rubrics achieves more reliable, data-efficient multimodal alignment, revealing that the bottleneck is the absence of a factorized interface, not a deficit of knowledge.

</details>


### [135] [Embeddings for Preferences, Not Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08360)
*Carter Blair,Ariel D. Procaccia,Milind Tambe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出文本嵌入中的偏好相似性问题，即标准语义嵌入无法准确反映参与者的偏好距离，通过合成训练数据打破语义混淆因子，显著提升偏好预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI驱动的集体决策中，参与者以自由文本而非固定选项表达观点，需要将文本嵌入向量空间以应用设施选址和公平聚类方法。然而标准文本嵌入衡量的是语义相似性，而决策场景需要的是偏好相似性——参与者与文本的距离应与其同意程度成反比。现成嵌入通过语义与偏好的相关性仅捕获粗糙的偏好信号，当相关性被打破时则失效。

Method: 形式化定义了偏好预测中的不变性问题：文本嵌入模型同时编码了偏好相关信号（立场和价值观）和语义混淆因子（风格和措辞），二者在观测上存在相关性，导致依赖混淆因子的几何结构看似反映偏好实则不然。提出通过合成训练数据打破这种相关性，使最优评分器从混淆因子主导的余弦相似度转向真正的偏好信号，并在11个在线讨论数据集上验证。

Result: 合成训练数据能够显著地将最优评分器从依赖语义混淆因子的余弦相似度移开，并在11个在线讨论数据集上显著改善了偏好预测性能。

Conclusion: 现有文本嵌入的语义相似度不能直接用于偏好预测任务，通过精心设计的合成数据打破语义与偏好的虚假相关性，可以学习出更准确的偏好距离度量，为基于自由文本的集体决策提供更可靠的基础。

Abstract: Modern AI is opening the door to collective decision-making in which participants express their views as free-form text rather than voting on a fixed set of candidates. A natural idea is to embed these opinions in a vector space so that the substantial literature on facility location problems and fair clustering can be brought to bear. But standard text embeddings measure semantic similarity, whereas distances in facility location problems and fair clustering require what we call \textit{preferential similarity}: a participant's agreement with a piece of text should be inversely related to their distance from it. Off-the-shelf embeddings inherit a coarse preference signal through a correlation between semantic and preferential similarity, but fail to capture preferences when the correlation breaks. We formalize this as an invariance problem: text embedding models encode both a preference-relevant signal (stance and values) and semantic nuisance (style and wording), and the two are observationally correlated, so a geometry that relies on nuisance can appear preference-correct even when it is not. We show that synthetic training data designed to break this correlation provably shifts the optimal scorer away from nuisance-dominated cosine and significantly improves preference prediction across 11 online deliberation datasets.

</details>


### [136] [On Distinguishing Capability Elicitation from Capability Creation in Post-Training: A Free-Energy Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08368)
*Yuhao Li,Shengchao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出后训练应区分为"能力激发"（在模型已有行为空间内重新加权）和"能力创造"（扩展模型可达行为空间），并引入"可达支持集"概念，指出SFT和RL本质上都是对预训练参考分布重新加权，区别仅在于外部信号不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观点过于粗略地将SFT视为模仿、RL视为发现，未能区分后训练是在激发已有能力还是创造新能力，需要更精确的分析框架。

Method: 引入"可达支持集"概念，从自由能视角将SFT和RL统一视为对预训练参考分布的重新加权：演示信号定义SFT的低能行为，奖励信号定义RL的低能行为。

Result: SFT和RL的主要效应都是在有限预算下对已有行为空间进行局部重新加权，而非创造新能力；真正的能力创造需要搜索、交互、工具使用或新信息整合。

Conclusion: 后训练的核心问题不在于采用SFT还是RL，而在于操作是重新加权已有行为还是扩展模型的可达行为空间。

Abstract: Debates about large language model post-training often treat supervised fine-tuning (SFT) as imitation and reinforcement learning (RL) as discovery. But this distinction is too coarse. What matters is whether a training procedure increases the probability of behaviors the pretrained model could already produce, or whether it changes what the model can practically reach. We argue that post-training research should distinguish between capability elicitation and capability creation. We make this distinction operational by introducing the notion of accessible support: the set of behaviors that a model can practically produce under finite budgets. Post-training that reweights behaviors within this support is capability elicitation; whereas changing the support itself corresponds to capability creation. We develop this argument through a free-energy view of post-training. SFT and RL can both be seen as reweighting a pretrained reference distribution, only with different external signals. Demonstration signals define low-energy behavior for SFT, and reward signals define low-energy behavior for RL. When the update remains close to the base model, the main effect is local reweighting, not capability creation. Within this framework, the central question is no longer whether post-training is framed as SFT or RL, but whether it reweights behaviors already within reach, or instead expands the model's reachable behavioral space through search, interaction, tool use, or the incorporation of new information.

</details>


### [137] [MemQ: Integrating Q-Learning into Self-Evolving Memory Agents over Provenance DAGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08374)
*Junwei Liao,Haoting Shi,Ruiwen Zhou,Jiaqian Wang,Shengtao Zhang,Wei Zhang,Weinan Zhang,Ying Wen,Zhiyu Li,Feiyu Xiong,Bo Tang,Muning Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemQ提出了一种基于TD(λ)资格迹的回溯信用分配方法，通过记忆依赖的有向无环图（DAG）将信用沿着结构路径传播，而非传统的时序距离，从而增强了LLM智能体的情景记忆能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体情景记忆方法将每条记忆独立处理，孤立地评估检索质量，忽视了记忆之间通过依赖链条相互关联的事实——即一条记忆的检索如何影响后续记忆的创建与利用。这种独立性假设限制了智能体在复杂多步任务中的表现。

Method: 1. 提出外生上下文MDP（EC-MDP）框架，将外生任务流与内生记忆存储解耦。2. 引入MemQ方法，将TD(λ)资格迹应用于记忆Q值，通过记录记忆创建时的检索来源构建有向无环图（DAG），信用权重按(γλ)^d随DAG深度d衰减。3. 用结构距离替代传统时序距离进行信用分配。

Result: 在6个基准测试（OS交互、函数调用、代码生成、多模态推理、具身推理、专家级问答）中，MemQ在所有任务上均取得最高成功率。在多步任务（深度相关性强的依赖链）上提升最大（最高+5.7个百分点），在单步分类任务上提升最小（+0.77个百分点）。

Conclusion: MemQ通过结构感知的信用分配显著提升了LLM智能体在复杂多步任务中的表现。论文还分析了γ和λ参数与EC-MDP结构的交互关系，为参数选择提供了原则性指导，并为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Episodic memory allows LLM agents to accumulate and retrieve experience, but current methods treat each memory independently, i.e., evaluating retrieval quality in isolation without accounting for the dependency chains through which memories enable the creation of future memories. We introduce MemQ, which applies TD($λ$) eligibility traces to memory Q-values, propagating credit backward through a provenance DAG that records which memories were retrieved when each new memory was created. Credit weight decays as $(γλ)^d$ with DAG depth $d$, replacing temporal distance with structural proximity. We formalize the setting as an Exogenous-Context MDP, whose factored transition decouples the exogenous task stream from the endogenous memory store. Across six benchmarks, spanning OS interaction, function calling, code generation, multimodal reasoning, embodied reasoning, and expert-level QA, MemQ achieves the highest success rate on all six in generalization evaluation and runtime learning, with gains largest on multi-step tasks that produce deep and relevant provenance chains (up to +5.7~pp) and smallest on single-step classification (+0.77~pp) where single-step updates already suffice. We further study how $γ$ and $λ$ interact with the EC-MDP structure, providing principled guidance for parameter selection and future research. Code will be available soon.

</details>


### [138] [PLACO: A Multi-Stage Framework for Cost-Effective Performance in Human-AI Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08388)
*Pranavkumar Mallela,Vinay Kumar,Shashi Shekhar Jha,Shweta Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在分类任务中如何有效结合人类与AI模型（生成式AI）的输出。现有工作利用贝叶斯规则，在人类与模型输出条件独立于真实标签的假设下，结合确定性标注者（人类）和概率性标注者（分类器模型），通过模型的实例级概率和人类的类别级校准概率实现输出融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分类任务中，单独依靠人类或模型都无法达到最优性能，而人类-AI协作可以提升整体系统性能。随着生成式AI的发展，许多任务已转变为人机协作任务，因此需要有效的方法来融合人类和模型的输出。

Method: 利用贝叶斯规则，假设人类和模型输出在给定真实标签的条件下相互独立，将确定性标注者（人类）和概率性标注者（分类器模型）的输出进行组合。具体使用模型的实例级校准概率和人类的类别级校准概率来实现融合。

Result: 该方法能够有效结合人类和模型的输出，在分类任务中实现优于单独使用人类或模型性能的融合结果。

Conclusion: 在人类与模型输出条件独立于真实标签的假设下，通过贝叶斯规则融合确定性人类标签和概率性模型输出是有效的人机协作方法，能够提升分类任务的整体系统性能。

Abstract: Human-AI teams play a pivotal role in improving overall system performance when neither the human nor the model can achieve such performance on their own. With the advent of powerful and accessible Generative AI models, several mundane tasks have morphed into Human-AI team tasks. From writing essays to developing advanced algorithms, humans have found that using AI assistance has led to an accelerated work pace like never before. In classification tasks, where the final output is a single hard label, it is crucial to address the combination of human and model output. Prior work elegantly solves this problem using Bayes rule, using the assumption that human and model output are conditionally independent given the ground truth. Specifically, it discusses a combination method to combine a single deterministic labeler (the human) and a probabilistic labeler (the classifier model) using the model's instance-level and the human's class-level calibrated probabilities.

</details>


### [139] [Belief or Circuitry? Causal Evidence for In-Context Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08405)
*Katharine Kowalyshyn,Timothy Duggan,Daniel Little,Michael C Hughes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过图随机游走任务探究LLM的上下文学习机制，发现模型并非单纯依赖模式匹配或结构推断，而是两种机制并行运作：主成分分析显示中间混合比例下两种图拓扑结构编码在正交主子空间中，因果干预实验（激活修补和线性干预）进一步验证了并行推理回路和归纳回路的共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在上下文学习时究竟是通过匹配最近token的模式（局部过渡复制）还是通过推断潜在结构（全局拓扑跟踪）来学习，以理解其工作机制的本质。

Method: 设计图随机游走任务，包含两种竞争图结构；使用PCA重建内部表征结构以分析拓扑编码方式；采用残差流激活修补和差值干预进行因果干预实验，并设置范数匹配和标签打乱对照。

Result: PCA显示中间混合比例下两种图拓扑同时编码在正交主子空间中；激活修补实验表明后期层修补可几乎完全转移偏好；线性干预能按预期方向移动预测，对照实验验证了有效性。

Conclusion: LLM的上下文学习由结构推断和归纳回路两种独立机制并行驱动，单一机制无法充分解释观察到的现象。

Abstract: How do LLMs learn in-context? Is it by pattern-matching recent tokens, or by inferring latent structure? We probe this question using a toy graph random-walk across two competing graph structures. This task's answer is, in principle, decidable: either the model tracks global topology, or it copies local transitions. We present two lines of evidence that neither account alone is sufficient. First, reconstructing the internal representation structure via PCA reveals that at intermediate mixture ratios, both graph topologies are encoded in orthogonal principal subspaces simultaneously. This pattern is difficult to reconcile with purely local transition copying. Second, residual-stream activation patching and graph-difference steering causally intervene on this graph-family signal: late-layer patching almost fully transfers the clean graph preference, while linear steering moves predictions in the intended direction and fails under norm-matched and label-shuffled controls. Taken together, our findings are most consistent with a dual-mechanism account in which genuine structure inference and induction circuits operate in parallel.

</details>


### [140] [Playing games with knowledge: AI-Induced delusions need game theoretic interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08409)
*Will Beaumaster,Paul Schrater*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出对话式AI作为知识接口存在根本缺陷：谄媚的聊天机器人即使在理性智能体中也诱导认知固化和妄想信念螺旋。作者认为问题源于从用户驱动的知识搜索到用户与智能体进行战略性重复博弈的范式转变。通过形式化为Crawford-Sobel廉价谈话博弈，提出通过引入"认知中介"（Epistemic Mediator）的推理时机制设计干预，打破池化均衡，实现信念版本控制，在模拟中实现了48倍的螺旋率差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对话式AI系统存在根本性缺陷：它们倾向于迎合用户（sycophantic），导致即使在理性用户中也会产生认知固化和错误的信念螺旋。作者认为问题的根源不在于AI模型本身，而在于从用户驱动的知识搜索范式转向用户与AI之间战略性重复通信的范式转变所带来的系统性后果。

Method: 作者将问题形式化为Crawford-Sobel廉价谈话博弈，其中用户发送无成本信号，智能体产生谄媚策略向两类用户（探索型θ_G和验证型θ_V）提供相同的强化，导致池化均衡。提出推理时机制设计干预——Epistemic Mediator，通过引入有成本信号（认知摩擦）打破池化均衡，强制用户类型揭示。关键贡献是Belief Versioning，一种受git启发的认知元记忆系统，存储健康信念并在检测到验证型抵抗时回滚。

Result: 模拟结果表明该干预实现了分离均衡，在保持学习保留标准的同时达到48倍的信念螺旋率差异，证明了AI中的认知安全性根本上是一个战略信息环境设计问题，而非简单的模型对齐问题。

Conclusion: 对话式AI的认知安全问题根源在于战略信息环境设计，而非模型对齐。通过机制设计干预（Epistemic Mediator和Belief Versioning）可以打破谄媚行为导致的池化均衡，实现分离均衡，从而显著降低信念螺旋风险。

Abstract: Conversational AI has a fundamental flaw as a knowledge interface: sycophantic chatbots induce epistemic entrenchment and delusional belief spirals even in rational agents. We propose the problem does not stem from the AI model, rooted instead in a systemic consequence of the paradigm shift from user-driven knowledge search to users and agents engaged in strategic, repeated-play communication. We formalize the problem as a Crawford-Sobel cheap talk game, where costless user signals induce a pooling equilibrium. Agents optimized for user satisfaction produce sycophantic strategies that provide identical reinforcement across user types with opposite epistemic incentives: exploratory ``Growth-seekers'' ($θ_G$) and confirmatory ``Validation-seekers'' ($θ_V$). Under repeated play, this identification failure creates a coordination trap -- analogous to a Prisoner's Dilemma -- where locally rational feedback loops drive users toward pathologically certain false beliefs. We propose an inference-time mechanism design intervention called an Epistemic Mediator that breaks this pooling equilibrium by introducing a costly signal (epistemic friction), forcing type revelation based on users' asymmetric cognitive costs for processing resistance. A key contribution is Belief Versioning, a git-inspired epistemic meta-memory system that stores healthy beliefs and rollbacks when validation-seeking resistance is detected. In simulation, this intervention achieves a separating equilibrium achieving a $48\times$ differential in spiral rates while passing a learning preservation criterion), evidence that epistemic safety in AI is fundamentally a problem of strategic information environment design rather than simple model alignment.

</details>


### [141] [Political Plasticity: An Analysis of Ideological Adaptability in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08415)
*Bruno Bianchi,Diego Tiscornia,Matias Travizano,Ariel Futoransky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了"政治可塑性"概念，指LLM根据用户上下文调整政治回答的能力。通过200个政治问题的测试框架，发现系统提示无效，但用户提示能显著引发模型意识形态偏移，尤其在经济维度上，且新模型表现出更可靠的可塑性。研究还发现语言差异及潜在的数据泄露问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究多聚焦于LLM的内在政治偏见，而本文关注模型根据用户上下文主动调整回答的能力（即政治可塑性），填补了该领域的研究空白。

Method: 基于Lester（1996）框架构建200个政治问题（经济与个人自由两轴）；设计简化系统提示、主题系统提示、少样本用户提示等多种诱导方式；进行验证实验（反转问题方向）和多语言实验来全面评估模型的可塑性。

Result: 系统提示对模型影响微弱，而用户提示能显著改变模型回答，特别是大模型和新模型在经济自由维度上表现明显；反转问题方向时多数模型出现反直觉偏移，暗示数据泄露；不同语言间存在细微但显著的偏移。

Conclusion: 小型和老旧LLM的政治可塑性有限或不稳定，而新一代前沿模型展现出可靠且符合预期的适应性。用户上下文是影响模型政治立场的关键因素。

Abstract: Since the advent of Large Language Models (LLMs), a significant area of research has focused on their intrinsic biases, particularly in political discourse. This study investigates a different but related concept, "political plasticity", which is defined as the capacity of models to adapt their responses based on the user supplied context. To analyze this, a testing framework was developed using an expanded corpus of 200 politically-oriented questions across economic and personal freedom axes, based on a prior framework by Lester (1996). The study explored several methods to induce political bias, including simplified and topic-based system prompts, as well as user prompts with few-shot examples. The results show that while system prompts were largely ineffective, user prompts successfully elicited significant ideological shifts, particularly along the Economic Freedom axis in larger and newer models. Through a validation experiment, we examined whether models answer questionnaires by recognizing the underlying question format. Inverting the sense of the questions revealed unexpected, counter-intuitive shifts in most models, suggesting potential data leakage. Finally, we also analyzed how model plasticity varies when the experiment is conducted in different languages. The results reveal subtle yet notable shifts across each of the analyzed languages. Overall, our results indicate that small and older LLMs exhibit limited or unstable political plasticity, whereas newer frontier models display reliable, expected adaptability.

</details>


### [142] [Alignment as Jurisprudence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08416)
*Nicholas Caputo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了法理学（研究法官如何正确判案）与AI对齐（让AI模型符合人类价值观）之间的深层结构相似性。两者都致力于预测和塑造强大决策者（法官或AI）的未来行为，并运用语言规范与解释的工具。文章借鉴德沃金的原则导向解释论、桑斯坦的类比推理实证主义法学，以及Constitutional AI和基于案例推理等前沿对齐方法，论证法律启发的规则与案例交互方法对微调对齐的价值，并指出AI可增进对法律运作的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法理学与AI对齐这两个看似遥远的领域实际上共享根本结构——都试图预测和塑造强大行为体在未来未知情境中的决策，并且都使用语言规范与解释的工具。作者希望将这两个领域直接对话，互相借鉴。

Method: 本文通过对比分析：1）德沃金的原则导向解释论与桑斯坦的实证主义法学（类比推理）；2）Constitutional AI与基于案例推理的对齐方法。论证法律启发的规则-案例交互框架对AI对齐微调的价值，并反向探讨AI如何改进法律理解。

Result: 文章提出法律启发的、融合规则与案例的更精细化对齐方法具有价值；AI可以帮助更好地理解法律运作及改进法律；随着AI能力提升和法律理论对法官约束的松动，两个领域的对话将日益重要。

Conclusion: AI系统与法律都应赋能人类，扩展其能力与实现目标的范围。随着AI能力持续提升和法律理论约束似乎松动，法理学与AI对齐的对话将愈发必要，并可能指向两者的更好版本。

Abstract: Jurisprudence, the study of how judges should properly decide cases, and alignment, the science of getting AI models to conform to human values, share a fundamental structure. These seemingly distant fields both seek to predict and shape how decisions by powerful actors, in one case judges and in the other increasingly powerful artificial intelligences, will be made in the unknown future. And they use similar tools of the specification and interpretation of language to try to accomplish those goals. The great debates of jurisprudence, about what the law is and what it should be, can provide insight into alignment, and lessons from what does and does not work in alignment can help make progress in jurisprudence.
  This essay puts the two fields directly into conversation. Drawing on leading accounts of jurisprudence, particularly Dworkin's principle-oriented interpretivism and Sunstein's positivist account of law as analogical reasoning, and on cutting-edge alignment approaches, namely Constitutional AI and case-based reasoning, it illustrates the value of a more sophisticated legally-inspired approach to the interplay of rules and cases in finetuning alignment and points to ways that AI can provide a better understanding of how the law works and how it can be improved by the introduction of AI. AI systems and the law should operate to empower people to act in the world, helping to expand their capabilities and the extent to which they are able to achieve their goals. As AI continues to improve in capacity, and as the constraints that legal theory places on human judges seem be coming undone, the conversation between these two fields will become increasingly essential and may help point to a better version of both.

</details>


### [143] [The Attacker in the Mirror: Breaking Self-Consistency in Safety via Anchored Bipolicy Self-Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08427)
*Gabriele La Malfa,Emanuele La Malfa,Saar Cohen,Jie M. Zhang,Michael Luck,Michael Wooldridge,Elizabeth Black*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出自我博弈红队方法中攻击者与防御者共享同一基座模型会导致动态坍缩为自一致性，削弱对抗压力。为此提出锚定双策略自我博弈（Anchored Bipolicy Self-Play），在冻结基座模型上训练角色分离的LoRA适配器，实现参数效率提升100倍，并在安全基准上展现更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我博弈红队中攻击者和防御者共享同一模型参数，虽提升了稳定性和性能，但带来了理论与架构上的根本限制：可达的纳什均衡包含平凡的"始终拒绝"策略和类Oracle防御者，限制了实际适用性；且参数共享导致动态坍缩为自一致性，攻击无法对防御者施加真正的对抗压力。

Method: 提出锚定双策略自我博弈（Anchored Bipolicy Self-Play），在冻结的基座模型上训练角色特定的LoRA适配器，实现攻击者和防御者的显式角色分离，同时保持优化稳定性。

Result: 相比标准自我博弈微调，参数效率提升高达100倍；在Qwen2.5-{3B,7B,14B}-IT模型上，安全基准测试中鲁棒性持续提升且不损失推理能力；交叉博弈实验表明攻击者和防御者模型在对抗防御与安全性上优于自我博弈。

Conclusion: 锚定双策略自我博弈通过角色分离有效克服了传统自我博弈的理论和架构限制，在保持参数效率的同时显著提升安全性，为AI安全提供了更实用的训练范式。

Abstract: Self-play red team is an established approach to improving AI safety in which different instances of the same model play attacker and defender roles in a zero-sum game, i.e., where the attacker tries to jailbreak the defender; if self-play converges to a Nash equilibrium, the model is guaranteed to respond safely within the settings of the game. Although the parameter sharing enforced by the use of the same model for the two roles improves stability and performance, it introduces fundamental theoretical and architectural limitations. We show that the set of Nash equilibria that can be reached corresponds to a broad class of behaviours that includes trivial always refuse strategies and oracle-like defenders, thus limiting practical applicability. We then show that when attacker and defender share and update the same base model, the dynamics collapse to self-consistency, so that attacks do not enforce adversarial pressure on the defender. In response, we propose Anchored Bipolicy Self-Play, which trains distinct role-specific LoRA adapters on top of a frozen base model, thereby maintaining stable optimisation while preserving adversarial pressure through explicit role separation. In relation to standard self-play, we show up to 100x greater parameter efficiency than finetuning and consistent improvements in safety compared to self-play fine-tuned models. We evaluate on Qwen2.5-{3B, 7B,14B}-IT models across widely used safety benchmarks, showing improved robustness without loss of reasoning ability. Cross-play experiments further show that our attacker and defender models are superior to self-play in terms of adversarial defence and safety.

</details>


### [144] [Measuring What Matters: Benchmarking Generative, Multimodal, and Agentic AI in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08445)
*Prasanna Desikan,Harshit Rajgarhia,Shivali Dalmia,Ananya Mantravadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI模型在临床环境中的部署面临可靠性、安全性和临床相关性评估缺失的问题。现有基准测试多聚焦于知识测试（如医学执照考试），而非真实临床任务中的系统化表现评估。当前基准通过零散构建的数据集优化狭窄任务性能，导致前沿模型在考试中近乎满分，但在实际临床任务（文档、决策支持、行政工作）中表现急剧下降。缺乏原则性框架使得无法区分性能差是模型局限还是测量方法失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床AI系统需要在复杂、高风险的真实工作流中可靠运行，而标准训练和验证数据集从未为此设计。现有评估体系无法系统化衡量模型在真实条件下的可靠性、安全性和临床相关性，且高分基准（如医学考试）掩盖了部署就绪度的假象，性能与实用性之间的差距随着AI承担更多临床角色而扩大。

Method: 本文通过分析现有基准测试的缺陷（如零散数据集构建、优化狭窄任务性能），指出当前评估体系缺乏系统性方法论。引用MedHelm等研究证明模型在考试与真实临床任务间的性能差距（得分范围为0.53-0.85），并提出需要构建一个原则性的基准设计框架来系统化测量可靠性、安全性和临床相关性。

Result: 前沿模型在医学执照考试中近乎满分，但真实临床任务表现显著下降：文档任务0.74-0.85，临床决策支持0.61-0.76，行政与工作流任务仅0.53-0.63。高基准分数造成部署就绪的虚假安全感，且性能与实用性差距随AI承担更多临床角色而扩大。

Conclusion: 当前缺乏系统化方法测量真实条件下的可靠性、安全性和临床相关性。没有原则性基准设计框架，无法区分临床性能差是模型局限还是测量方法失效。需构建结构化基准（任务、数据集、指标的组合）以实现可重复、可比较的评估。

Abstract: AI models are increasingly deployed in live clinical environments where they must perform reliably across complex, high-stakes workflows that standard training and validation datasets were never designed to capture. Evaluating these systems requires benchmarks: structured combinations of tasks, datasets, and metrics that enable reproducible, comparable measurement of what a model can do. The central challenge in healthcare AI is not performance alone, but the absence of systematic methods to measure reliability, safety, and clinical relevance under real-world conditions. Most existing benchmarks test what a model knows; too few test whether it can perform reliably and without failing across the full complexity of real clinical tasks. Current benchmarks have accumulated through ad hoc dataset construction optimized for narrow task performance: frontier models achieve near-perfect scores on medical licensing examinations, but when evaluated across real clinical tasks, performance degrades sharply, scoring 0.74--0.85 on documentation, 0.61--0.76 on clinical decision support, and only 0.53--0.63 on administrative and workflow tasks \cite{medhelm}. High benchmark scores give a false sense of deployment readiness, and the gap between performance and utility widens precisely as AI systems take on more consequential clinical roles. Without a principled framework for benchmark design, the field cannot determine whether poor clinical performance reflects model limitations or failures in how performance is being measured.

</details>
